- **shouldislab**: Use this skill when the user asks about Pokemon card grading, value, or whether a card is worth grading/slabbing. Triggers: 'should I grade', 'should I slab', 'is this card worth grading', 'pokemon card value', 'PSA grade', 'card worth', 'grade or not', 'shouldislab', 'slab check'. Looks up card data, pricing (raw vs graded), estimates grading ROI, and gives a clear slab-or-skip verdict. Do NOT use for: non-Pokemon TCG cards, general Pokemon game questions, deck building, or card generation. ([KeWang0622/shouldislab](https://github.com/KeWang0622/shouldislab/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **shouldn-t-android-accountmanager-store-4940f5b82bc4889b**: Shows how to fix shouldn't Android AccountManager Store OAuth Tokens on a Per-App/UID Basis?. Use it when you hit this exact problem. - **shouying-precheck**: 医药首营资料预审（公开法规版）。触发词：首营预审、预审资料、审一下首营、首营资料齐不齐、供应商资料预审。硬流程：封面级清点（不读字段值）→第一条回复只能是确认消息且不含任何结论词，得到使用人答复前禁止输出任何结果→品种归属表→按范围审（跨文件核对填空表+数字多源校验：文本层→视觉→放大/解码器→EAN-13校验位自算）→自检门8断言→按template.html模板填空出HTML报告+一段话摘要。一个首营案一份主报告（供货商=开票方）。预审≠质管法定审核。 ([ngseayoung-eng/shouying-precheck](https://github.com/ngseayoung-eng/shouying-precheck/tree/HEAD/shouying-precheck))
